- **gws-tips**: Tips, gotchas, and recipes for the gws CLI (googleworkspace/cli). Read before running any gws command. Grammar is `gws &lt;service&gt; &lt;resource&gt; &lt;method&gt; --params '{JSON}'`; every API argument (fileId, documentId, spreadsheetId, calendarId, range, fields, q) goes inside `--params`, NOT as a flag like `--fileId`. Discover params with `gws schema &lt;service.resource.method&gt;`, not `--help`. Also covers Doc comment anchored text, stray download files, and other API quirks.
